## WU1C2B2 — Validation error redaction (REMEDIATED)

**Branch:** `feat/receipt-media-ingestion-wu01c2b2-validation-redaction` | **Next:** WU2A1

### Prior failure history (preserved for traceability, not erased)

The first WU1C2B2 apply reached focused Jest **116 failed / 27 passed** with `TypeError: state.mainstay.tracer.value is not a function` thrown from `joi/lib/validator.js`. Root cause: `stripContextValueRecursive(detail.context)` walks the Joi `Report` retained at `detail.context.error`, follows `error._state.mainstay.tracer.value`, and DELETES Joi's `internals.ignore.value`. Subsequent validations (which share the same tracer) then throw before the first coercion step. The corrective prescription applied in that attempt (rename `envValidationSchema` to `innerEnvValidationSchema`, drop the redundant alias) was a no-op against the actual preserved tree — the rename was already in place and the alias was already removed — so it could not address the redactor ↔ tracer mutation.

### Remediation applied under explicit human-authorized proceed token

| Phase       | Evidence                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| RED         | Preserved failed evidence `sha256:18850f33e5c96d0c688f68a483075022f17cd7f87d091d4e3f0ed8d59b97cea7` (116 failed / 27 passed) confirmed the redactor ↔ tracer corruption across the entire 143-test matrix; the failure is reproducible on every test that triggers a custom re-throw followed by any subsequent `envValidationSchema.validate(env, options)` call.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| GREEN       | Applied the orchestrator-prescribed surgical fix inside `stripContextValueRecursive`: immediately after `delete record.value;` and before the recursive key iteration, added `if ('error' in record) { delete record.error; }`. This both (a) prevents the redactor from descending into Joi's internal `Report` and corrupting `state.mainstay.tracer.value`, and (b) removes an internal object that could itself retain secret-bearing state. The fix is scoped to the single touched block inside `stripContextValueRecursive`; schema declarations, the facade, the spec, comments, and every other redaction behavior are untouched.                                                                                            |
| TRIANGULATE | Re-ran the full existing test matrix (no rewrite, no expansion) — the prior `redactValidationError` reproducer and every adjacent assertion pass; the `JSON.stringify(details)` and `String(error)` checks on credential-sentinel and keyring-sentinel values confirm redaction while the tracer mutation is now blocked at the source.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| REFACTOR    | No further consolidation needed; the targeted removal is a minimal, local edit. A pre-existing malformed-indentation artifact inside `stripContextValueRecursive` (line `if ('value' in record) {` printed at column 0 in the prior preserved state) was auto-corrected by the editor lint hook (4 spaces → 2 spaces) — this is whitespace inside the touched block and changes no behavior.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| VERIFY      | `pnpm test -- src/config/env.validation.spec.ts` → **143 passed / 0 failed / 143 total**; `pnpm exec eslint src/config/env.validation.ts src/config/env.validation.spec.ts` → clean; `pnpm exec prettier --check src/config/env.validation.ts src/config/env.validation.spec.ts` → all matched files use Prettier code style; `git diff --check` → clean; candidate TypeScript diagnostics scoped to the two files → **0 errors** (140 pre-existing errors elsewhere in the codebase, none in `env.validation.ts` or its spec); `git diff --numstat -- src/config/env.validation.ts src/config/env.validation.spec.ts` → `102 0 spec.ts`, `49 1 env.validation.ts`, total **152 changed lines** (within the 110–160 review boundary). |

**Implementation/tests:** `env.validation.ts` 49 additions / 1 deletion (the surgical `delete record.error` plus the indentation normalization inside `stripContextValueRecursive`); `env.validation.spec.ts` 102 additions / 0 deletions → **152 changed lines** (within the 110–160 review boundary).
**File hashes:**

- `src/config/env.validation.ts` `sha256:4b304e0b7c2361a9b8b9ab98e991ede153af45947aa9f591d04a2b6bb2f7f18e`
- `src/config/env.validation.spec.ts` `sha256:fc134c3b440bb18d85e1e02e96f3097822bece376a8a21c284131563fa46adb8`
- `openspec/changes/receipt-media-ingestion/tasks.md` `sha256:c839d869e6cb5d80065475016efee2802955b9033f0351e026a4461a9db153b6`

**Remediation evidence revision:** `sha256:fc9c2d00c0c4f5efa9300842a5ce1dbb5f02124419a5c7498dad317d34ffe33b` — distinct from failed evidence `sha256:18850f33e5c96d0c688f68a483075022f17cd7f87d091d4e3f0ed8d59b97cea7`.
**Native closure:** the failed evidence was remediated in place; the prior failure history is preserved in this section for traceability, not erased. No commit, push, PR, lockfile mutation, or backend/secret access occurred. The WU1C2B2 work unit is GREEN and ready for verify/sync/archive by the parent.

The maintainer authorized preserving that candidate for compaction and correction without changing functional scope. `tasks.md` now sets the WU2A1 final review boundary to an absolute 300–400 changed lines and requires verification against that cap. Native reset revision: `sha256:31d5dc52ad0521fc6a0337a6e8fc5bd76f2da140403ae991301ed75ee85953d4`. No commit, push, PR, merge, source-file edit, or authoritative test result was produced.

### WU2A1 verification-command remediation note (superseded by the completion section below)

Independent verification passed the focused Testcontainers suite at **49/49 tests**, Prettier format check, `git diff --check`, the scoped TypeScript build over the WU2A1 surface, the scope/security inspection, and the primary LSP diagnostics check. The original migration ESLint command (`pnpm exec eslint migrations/2000000000000_receipt_media.js ...`) was invalid: the CommonJS migration file is outside the TypeScript project service, so ESLint could not lint it. The corrected syntax check for the migration is `node --check migrations/2000000000000_receipt_media.js`, recorded in WU2A1 step 5; its result is now recorded in the completion section below — this earlier note no longer represents WU2A1 status. ## WU2B2B remediation — parent-gate TS1128 correction rerun (audited)

**Trigger:** the parent gate independently reproduced two blocking diagnostics against the submitted evidence revision `sha256:6e94a21f…`: `receipt-media-store.port.ts:82:1 TS1128` and `postgres-receipt-media.store.ts:280:1 TS1128` (confirmed by pi-lens). This formally **invalidates the prior record's "syntax already clean on arrival (0 primary TypeScript errors)" claim and the prior GREEN evidence table**: that claim was asserted without a fresh diagnostic rerun bound to the submitted bytes and is hereby corrected.

**Forensic finding:** the live working tree is byte-identical to the failed revision (all three file sha256s and the unified source diff digest `sha256:6e94a21f…` recomputed and matched), yet the two reported diagnostics do **not** reproduce against those exact bytes: both files end cleanly (81 and 279 lines — the reported positions 82:1 and 280:1 are past EOF), braces are balanced (13/13, 43/43), per-file `tsc --noEmit` exits 0, and project-wide `tsc -p tsconfig.build.json --incremental false` exits 0. Stale diagnostic caches were found and purged as the plausible phantom-error mechanism: `dist/tsconfig.build.tsbuildinfo` and `dist/tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` (gitignored build artifacts, likely imprinted by an earlier broken pre-compaction write). The surgical brace correction is therefore a confirmed **no-op on the live bytes** — no source edit was required or made.

| Per-file primary diagnostics          | `tsc --noEmit` (nodenext/ES2023, skipLibCheck) over port + store                                                                                                      | 0 errors, exit 0                                                                                     |

### TDD Cycle Evidence

| Task                   | Test file                                                            | Layer     | Safety net                                                                                                          | RED                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | GREEN                            | TRIANGULATE                                                                                                                                                            | REFACTOR                                                                                                            |

| WU4A remediation (RM2) | `src/receipt-media/infrastructure/media-structure.validator.spec.ts` | Pure unit | Entry-tree focused run was already green at 42/42 because the timed-out actor's minimal guards were present on disk | Preserved parent evidence `sha256:e21973bd6fe955d6eadd739d262d38d63f7cd2b37a9e5a893a7393d892bfd6b9`: exactly three intended failures—PNG without IDAT, JPEG SOF after SOS, and JPEG SOS followed by EOI without entropy. No new pre-write RED is claimed. | Final focused Jest: 43/43 passed | Real zlib data split across IDAT chunks; SOF-after-SOS with and without entropy; empty, restart-only, second-scan, stuffed-byte, and restart-marker entropy boundaries | Replaced duplicated JPEG throw helpers with readable boolean frame/scan predicates; final focused test stayed 43/43 |

**Implementation:** PNG completion now requires an observed IDAT before IEND. JPEG rejects SOS immediately until a valid SOF is present, requires non-empty entropy for every scan, counts stuffed `FF 00` as entropy data, and rejects restart-marker-only entropy. Positive PNG data is one valid zlib stream split across IDAT chunks. The positive 1×1 grayscale JPEG includes a quantization table, valid DC and AC Huffman tables, valid SOF/SOS, and entropy; independent in-memory ImageMagick `identify` checks recognized both fixtures as 1×1 images.

### Static WU4B1A requirement confirmation (validated against the preserved source, no rewrite)

- **HTTPS only / no userinfo / no query / no fragment / default-port rule:** `validateMetaMediaOrigin` rejects non-`https:`, non-empty `username`/`password`/`search`/`hash`; implicit port accepted only when the effective allowed port is 443, and an explicit non-production `testPort` seam must be written out in the URL.
- **Normalized label-boundary allowlist:** entries lowercased, leading-dot marker stripped, exact or whole-label `.entry` suffix matching; `notgraph.facebook.com`, `graph.facebook.com.media.evil.example`, and subdomain-adjacent near-misses rejected while `lookaside.graph.facebook.com` is accepted; case/leading-dot normalization proven in the spec.
- **All A/AAAA globally public, including mapped IPv6:** `isPublicAddress` requires `ipaddr.js` `unicast` range and decodes IPv4-mapped IPv6 before re-checking (`::ffff:10.0.0.5` rejected, `::ffff:8.8.8.8` accepted); every resolved address must be public and an empty resolver result rejects.
- **Safe resolver/policy errors:** all parse/policy/resolver failures map to the fixed safe `MetaMediaError` `META_TRANSPORT`/`NETWORK_FAILURE` with no URL, host, or error-detail leakage; spec asserts exact category/code/message.
- **Hostname-bound pinned lookup (scalar and `options.all`):** `pinnedLookup` serves only the validated address set for the bound hostname, fails closed with `EMETAPIN` on any other hostname, round-robins scalar callbacks, and returns the full list for `options.all: true`.
- **Normal TLS verification:** the lookup never rewrites `servername` or `checkServerIdentity`; the spec proves option objects pass through unmutated.
- **No forbidden behavior:** no HTTP request, no bearer, no client file, no redirect, no stream/temp/hash/MIME handling anywhere in the policy source.

## WU4B1A — URL delimiter and DNS family remediation (WU4B1A-REM-1)

**Branch:** `feat/receipt-media-ingestion-wu04b1-meta-origin` | **Scope:** the two WU4B1A policy files only (max 100 changed lines, final cap 290 total).

### Failed evidence being remediated (named, not erased)

`sha256:fcdaeb44d80384ec1622611dd6cdfc150ec98f72e9fe9b1fac64510490d52e36` — the preserved RED state on the same work unit: focused Jest on `meta-media-origin.policy.spec.ts` showed exactly **6 failed / 31 passed (37 total)**, re-confirmed in this session before any production edit:

1. `rejects unsafe origin https://graph.facebook.com?` — trailing delimiter-only query erased by WHATWG normalization (`parsed.search === ''`), so the delimiter-only URL was accepted.
2. `rejects unsafe origin https://graph.facebook.com#` — same for a trailing delimiter-only fragment.
3. `isPublicAddress › declared family consistency` (three failing table rows) — `8.8.8.8` as 6, `2606:4700::1111` as 4, and `::ffff:8.8.8.8` as 4 all returned `true` because the declared family parameter was ignored.
4. `rejects resolver records with conflicting declared family` — resolver records with swapped declared families were accepted since only address syntax was checked.

### Remediation cycle (RED preserved → GREEN → TRIANGULATE → REFACTOR)

| Phase       | Evidence                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ----------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| RED         | Preserved failed evidence `sha256:fcdaeb44d80384ec1622611dd6cdfc150ec98f72e9fe9b1fac64510490d52e36`; no new RED cases were added before production correction (6 failed / 31 passed re-confirmed at session start).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| GREEN       | In `meta-media-origin.policy.ts`: (a) `validateMetaMediaOrigin` now tests the **raw** URL for any literal `?`/`#` before `new URL` and rejects via the fixed safe `MetaMediaError` — a trailing delimiter-only query/fragment can no longer be normalized away (any literal delimiter means query/fragment is present, so no previously accepted origin is affected); (b) `isPublicAddress(address, family?)` rejects a record when the declared resolver family does not match the parsed address kind (IPv4 → 4, IPv6 including IPv4-mapped → 6), and `validateMetaMediaOrigin` passes each `entry.family` through; correct IPv4-mapped IPv6 treatment is preserved (mapped addresses stay kind-IPv6, decoded, and re-checked for global unicast). |
| TRIANGULATE | Existing adjacent-case coverage re-proven: delimiter-bearing `?truncate=true`/`#fragment` still rejected; exact/443/subdomain/seam origins still accepted; mapped-public `::ffff:8.8.8.8` with family 6 still resolves while mapped-private and family 4 variants reject; one-private-among-public rejects; new adjacent triangulation row `https://graph.facebook.com/media?#` (delimiter-only query+fragment on a path URL, previously accepted) now rejects. Focused Jest: **38 passed / 0 failed / 38 total**.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| REFACTOR    | Spec-only consolidation, no assertion deleted: the two `isPublicAddress` `it.each` tables merged into one 19-row table (15 classification rows widened to explicit `undefined`-family 3-tuples plus the 4 declared-family rows), and the `rejects` helper now uses type-inferred default parameters matching the existing `accepts` helper style. Policy went 123 → 129 lines (+6); spec 167 → 159 (−8); **total 288 ≤ 290 cap**.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

## WU4B1B — error-format boundary correction (bounded correction unit)

**Branch:** `feat/receipt-media-ingestion-wu04b1b-metadata-request` | **Base HEAD:** `c97a5cf` | **Authority:** parent work unit `WU4B1B-error-format-boundary-correction`, token `sha256:91873eb06a2f1715c3219985d2622afa190650aa27db6089bbe4a0fdd37a2cc0`, max 100 changed lines. Owned surfaces only: the B1 client pair plus WU4B1.1/WU4B1.2 checkboxes and this append.

### Prior failing evidence (named, not erased)

At session start (pre-edit, re-confirmed) the preserved WU4B1B candidate stood at client **147 lines** + spec **198 lines** = 345, Prettier reported **20 spec formatting issues**, and focused Jest was **29 passed / 2 failed / 31 total**:

1. `maps transport failure ERR_CANCELED to the fixed safe ABORTED error` — received `NETWORK_FAILURE`, expected `ABORTED`.
2. `maps transport failure ECONNABORTED to the fixed safe TIMEOUT error` — received `NETWORK_FAILURE`, expected `TIMEOUT`.

Root cause: the spec's rejection-shape ternary (`typeof httpError === 'string' ? httpError : Object.assign(new Error('boom'), { code: httpError })`) was dead code — every table row is a string, so every rejection was a **plain code string**, while the client's `mapTransportError` only duck-typed `.code` on **objects**. Plain-string and synthetic rejections therefore fell through to `NETWORK_FAILURE`.

### Correction cycle (RED preserved → GREEN → TRIANGULATE → REFACTOR)

| Phase       | Evidence                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| RED         | Prior failing evidence re-confirmed at session start before any production edit: 2 failed / 29 passed with exactly the two boundary failures above; Prettier check failed the spec (20 issues); client+spec at 345 lines.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| GREEN       | In `meta-media.client.ts`: `mapTransportError` now classifies **duck-typed on the transport code only, with no Axios brand check**: a plain string rejection is its own code; an object rejection's `code` is read only when it is a string; `ERR_CANCELED` → `META_TRANSPORT`/`ABORTED`, `ECONNABORTED` → `META_TRANSPORT`/`TIMEOUT`, everything else → `META_TRANSPORT`/`NETWORK_FAILURE`. `MetaMediaError` passthrough unchanged. Plain strings and synthetic Error-like rejections now classify exactly like branded `AxiosError` rejections.                       |
| TRIANGULATE | The transport `it.each` table keeps every existing row and assertion and now exercises **both rejection shapes per row** (plain string code and `Object.assign(new Error('boom'), { code })`), so the same code classifies identically as string or synthetic error; `ECONNREFUSED`/`ETIMEDOUT`/`socket went away` still reject as `NETWORK_FAILURE`; all prior status/body/policy/agent assertions unchanged. Focused Jest: **31 passed / 0 failed / 31 total**.                                                                                                       |
| REFACTOR    | Honest consolidations only, no assertion deleted and no code-golf: (1) folded the single-use `transportCode` helper into `mapTransportError` (comment retained, expanded to name the no-brand-check rule); (2) inlined the `rejecting` resolver array into its `for…of` loop; (3) flattened the malformed-body `it.each` rows from tuples to bare values (Jest wraps non-array rows; identical inputs, titles, and assertions) letting Prettier collapse the table; (4) named the two rejection shapes as `rejections` and looped over them. Prettier-clean throughout. |

## WU4B1B — HTTP disposition remediation (bounded correction unit)

**Branch:** `feat/receipt-media-ingestion-wu04b1b-metadata-request` | **Base HEAD:** `c97a5cf` | **Authority:** parent work unit `WU4B1B HTTP-disposition remediation`, token `sha256:714a1ea7b9936bb247119a9155e1b5de85e729dac58f0afd1ad1d6e183c41b9c`, max 120 changed lines. Owned surfaces only: `meta-media.port.ts` (fixed safe union), `meta-media.client.ts`, `meta-media.client.spec.ts`, one WU4B1B remediation row in `tasks.md`, this append.

**Finding remediated:** the independently failed evidence `sha256:9c0e254c11b9162ebe3e3a168cfaeba57ee9b2ae3b1844f711cb8273216631fd` showed every non-2xx status and every malformed metadata body collapsing into `NETWORK_FAILURE`, conflating retryable HTTP dispositions with permanent ones. Design §line 327 requires retryable = network failure, 408, 429, 5xx and permanent = 4xx other than 408/429 plus missing/invalid metadata.

| GREEN       | Extended the fixed safe `MetaMediaErrorCode` union with `'HTTP_RETRYABLE' \| 'HTTP_PERMANENT'` (port, +2 lines, reported separately); added `httpStatusError()` (408/429/`>=500` retryable, all other non-2xx permanent) and mapped the metadata status check and the missing/invalid-body throw (`HTTP_PERMANENT`)                                                | `pnpm test -- src/receipt-media/infrastructure/meta-media.client.spec.ts` → **33 passed / 33 total / 0 failed**                                                                                                                                                                              |
| TRIANGULATE | Adjacent boundaries inside the tables: 408 (retryable) vs 409 (permanent); 428 (permanent) vs 429 (retryable); 499 (permanent) vs 500 (retryable); representative 5xx 500/502/503/504 all retryable; unfollowed 3xx 302/304/307/308 all permanent; 7 malformed bodies (including `null` and missing/empty/non-string `url`) all permanent, never network-retryable | Consolidated into one shared `expectRejection` proof (one composed hop, fixed safe error, single bearer, agent teardown) with readable flat tables; network failures remain `NETWORK_FAILURE` (5-case transport table unchanged)                                                             |

## WU4B2 — manual redirect download transport (preserved-candidate completion under human-authorized cap revision)

**Branch:** `feat/receipt-media-ingestion-wu04b2-redirect-download` | **Base HEAD:** `0169ba9` | **Authority:** human explicitly authorized the honest cap revision from 340 to 400 changed client/spec lines (parent token `sha256:97ba23d122408bf737f05f9f957f63e94f0f407e8f5b6f9c22f29972d0b79cf1`, max 400; parent owns settle). Owned surfaces only: the B2 client pair, the WU4B2 forecast row, WU4B2.1/WU4B2.2 checkboxes, and this append.

**Starting state:** the preserved candidate stood at client **277 lines** + spec **454 lines**; `git diff --numstat HEAD` = client 122/1 + spec 271/1 = **395 changed lines**. The executor first ran the full check battery to establish exact state; no genuine failure or incompleteness was found, so **no production code, test, or scope change was made** — this slice is a verification and settlement completion of the preserved candidate.

### Requirement reconciliation (all WU4B2 requirements proven by the spec, no gaps)

| Requirement                                                        | Evidence                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Metadata hop then authenticated streaming-response download        | `resolveDownloadUrl` composes/validates/pins the metadata hop and returns the download URL; `downloadStream` issues `responseType: 'stream'` GETs with `Authorization: Bearer …` per hop                                                                    |
| `proxy:false`, auto-redirect disabled                              | Every hop config asserts `proxy: false, maxRedirects: 0` (`expectHopDefaults` over calls[0] and followed hops)                                                                                                                                              |
| Signal and download timeout per hop                                | `signal: request.signal` and `timeout: 20000` asserted on every issued hop                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Origin/DNS revalidated and newly pinned before bearer at every hop | Loop body calls `validateMetaMediaOrigin` then `createAgent(pinnedLookup(...))` before any request/bearer; `re-resolves the redirect target per hop before its request and bearer` proves hop 2 resolve failure yields no hop-2 request and no hop-2 bearer |
| Only 301/302/303/307/308 followed manually                         | `it.each([301, 302, 303, 307, 308])` followed as fresh revalidated hops; `it.each([300, 304, 305, 306, 309])` never followed, one hop, safe permanent rejection                                                                                             |
| Relative/absolute Location resolved then revalidated               | `resolveRedirectTarget` resolves via `new URL(location, current)`; the resolved target re-enters the loop and is revalidated/repinned; absolute-target and relative-target cases both covered                                                               |
| At most three redirects                                            | Three redirects (four requests) succeed with exact URL sequence; a fourth redirect is rejected (`HTTP_PERMANENT`, four requests)                                                                                                                            |
| Fourth/missing/malformed/other-status safe rejection               | Fourth-redirect case; missing/empty/non-string/unparseable Location table; non-redirect status tables (400/404 permanent, 408/429/500 retryable) — all fixed safe `MetaMediaError` with leak regex `LEAKY` over `JSON.stringify(err)`                       |
| No request or bearer to rejected target                            | Disallowed absolute target `rebind.attacker.example` → `NETWORK_FAILURE` with exactly one call (the previous hop) and one bearer; policy-before-request ordering asserted                                                                                   |
| Redirect/error agents destroyed                                    | Every non-success hop destroys its agent exactly once (per-hop `destroyFns` assertions across redirect-later-fails, transport failure, status rejection tables)                                                                                             |
| Final agent held by explicit idempotent release                    | `MetaDownloadHandle.release` destroys exactly once across repeated calls; final-hop agent untouched while the stream is consumable                                                                                                                          |
| Ordinary TLS naming preserved                                      | `defaultAgentFactory` sets only `lookup`; no `servername`/`checkServerIdentity` override (asserted)                                                                                                                                                         |
| No secret/URL/host/IP/ID/body leakage                              | All rejection paths run `safeError`, asserting the fixed `receipt-media:META_TRANSPORT/<code>` message and `LEAKY` sentinel absence                                                                                                                         |
| No B3 behavior                                                     | Stream body passed through untouched; no MIME agreement, byte/hash, or temp-file code or tests exist in the slice                                                                                                                                           |

## WU4B3B2R1A — remediation three-way split, first slice (void progress proof + descriptor-safe close fixture on the preserved base failure matrix)

**Branch:** `feat/receipt-media-ingestion-wu04b3b2-failure-cleanup` | **Base:** exact `418829f090f0877ced1dec0c4c1515dbd143e8f8` | **Disposition: applied and verified green at 299/400 changed lines; awaiting parent settlement (native attempt lifecycle untouched — parent owns token `sha256:7cdbc2fe…`).**

### Authorization and partition (human-authorized reset + three-way sequential split R1A → R1B → R2)

The human maintainer explicitly authorized resetting the R1 remediation candidate (422 lines, revision `sha256:4b36bf54…`, blocked solely on budget) and splitting it three ways. This slice, **R1A**, keeps the already verified base WU4B3B2 failure matrix and only the two verifier-remediation deltas; everything else from the R1 candidate is truthfully deferred.

**Removed/deferred to WU4B3B2R1B (safe cleanup projection — restored to settled WU4B3B2 behavior, not deleted as lost work):**

- client: the `failedStep` helper; the success-path `cleanup` unlink-failure `.catch` projection (back to the plain idempotent `fs.promises.rm(tempPath, { force: true })`); the catch-flow restructure that surfaced failure-path close/unlink failures — the technical cleanup returned to the settled swallowed `finally` shape (`temp.close().catch(() => undefined)` + non-recursive `rm(...).catch(() => undefined)` after `handle?.release()`), which also structurally removes the `no-unsafe-finally` surface.
- spec: the three cleanup-projection tests (`surfaces FILE_IO_FAILURE when failure-path close fails and still unlinks`, `surfaces FILE_IO_FAILURE when failure-path unlink fails and leaks no temp detail`, `projects a real cleanup unlink failure to the fixed safe FILE_IO_FAILURE projection`); the `OVERFLOW` overflow-buffers constant and `dirSwapFactory` seam used only by those tests; the `safeError` `return err` and `expectRejectedFile` `{ created, err }` harness returns added only for those tests; the remediation-only `29` over-length entry in the write-progress `it.each` (the base four regressions `0/-1/1.5/MAX_SAFE_INTEGER+1` and the settled `writeAll` over-length guard are preserved).

**Preserved base WU4B3B2 failure matrix (untouched):** response Content-Type missing/unsupported/mismatching rejections and parameterized acceptance; Content-Length malformed/empty/negative/fractional/non-string/unsafe/over-limit/final-count-mismatch rejections and present-matching/absent acceptance independent of `providerDeclaredBytes`; malformed JPEG/PNG structure rejections; real mid-stream stream error and `ERR_CANCELED` stop-and-cleanup; already-active-abort rejection with no download hop and no temp; real-fs open/write/close/read-back failure seams mapping to `FILE_IO_FAILURE`; source destroy, single final release, full partial unlink, and the suite-level residue guard.

**Preserved R1A verifier-remediation deltas (the only additions beyond the base matrix):**

1. **Genuine void `FileHandle.write` progress:** `voidProgressFactory` reports `undefined` from a real factory seam without touching the real write path; `writeAll` rejects fail-closed with `META_TRANSPORT/FILE_IO_FAILURE` without looping forever and without leaking detail. RED provenance inherited truthfully from the R1 remediation cycle (the reverted-loop RED run: partial-write success + four zero/invalid-progress regressions failed 5/98 before GREEN); the historical oversized `7eefdd` tree is explicitly NOT used as RED evidence.
2. **Descriptor-safe close-failure fixture:** `closedFdFactory` explicitly closes the real `r+` FileHandle (and the factory-owned handle) before producing the real EBADF via `fs.closeSync(fd)`, so no descriptor is ever left to the garbage collector — the 'close' row of the real-fs failure matrix proves a genuine close failure with zero fd-GC/deprecation warnings.

**R2 stays pending:** no `onAbort` listener, no real mid-stream `AbortController.abort()` transition, and no related test exists in this tree; WU5+ untouched.

## WU4B3B2R1B — remediation three-way split, second slice (safe cleanup projection)

**Branch:** `feat/receipt-media-ingestion-wu04b3b2-failure-cleanup` | **Base:** exact `fbe9bd11fe99b5d6b597fb7d2e5449e69abed775` (R1A settled) | **Disposition: applied, independently rechecked and verified green at 207/400 changed lines; R1B remediated and green, awaiting independent reverification and parent settlement (native attempt ordinal 119 lifecycle untouched — parent owns active token `sha256:3ba117d56a80abdb6f6e79d9c70141ec5fbf9b74e657117b2b6fd5f5c966c4d3`; failed evidence revision being remediated: `sha256:6f10040db0385190c92232448df6db223f81593dea4c932f20f1f8be002a3f99`).**

### Restored/implemented R1B content (the deferred R1 partition, reconstructed from the documented R1 history, not from the discarded candidate bytes)

- client: the `failedStep` helper (`step().then(() => false, () => true)`, Prettier-required argument shape); the success-path caller-owned `cleanup()` now projects a real unlink failure through `ioStep` to the fixed safe `META_TRANSPORT/FILE_IO_FAILURE` while staying idempotent (`force: true`); the failure-path cleanup moved out of the swallowing `finally` into the explicit catch flow — it attempts close then unlink even after one step fails, captures each real step failure via `failedStep`, surfaces exactly one fixed safe `META_TRANSPORT/FILE_IO_FAILURE` when any cleanup step failed (a caller can never believe a temp file was removed when it was not), preserves the original safe domain error via `mapTransportError` when cleanup succeeds, destroys the source, releases the final agent exactly once, performs no recursive cleanup, and structurally removes the `no-unsafe-finally` surface (`succeeded`/`finally` removed).
- spec: the three cleanup-projection tests (`surfaces FILE_IO_FAILURE when failure-path close fails and still unlinks`, `surfaces FILE_IO_FAILURE when failure-path unlink fails and leaks no temp detail`, `projects a real cleanup unlink failure to the fixed safe FILE_IO_FAILURE projection`); the `OVERFLOW` overflow-buffers constant and `dirSwapFactory` real-unlink-failure seam (close then same-path non-empty-directory swap, so the pipeline's own `rm` fails genuinely); `safeError` now returns the projected `MetaMediaError` and `expectRejectedFile` returns `{ created, err }`; the remediation-only `29` over-length entry restored to the write-progress `it.each` (upper bound `progress > remaining`; the settled `writeAll` over-length guard — passes pre-GREEN as triangulation, not RED). The two directory-swapping tests own their swapped paths in `finally` (recursive `rm` + `expectUnlinked`), so even RED failures released every test-created resource deterministically.
- client (source-destroy-exactly-once remediation, added on top of the cleanup projection): the catch-path teardown now guards the explicit destroy — `if (source !== undefined && !source.destroyed) source.destroy()` — because once the `for await` loop has consumed the source, Node's async-iterator completion (abrupt overflow throw, mid-stream error, or normal end) has already destroyed it and a second unconditional destroy re-invoked the public `destroy()`; spec: the three destroy-counting tests (`destroys the source exactly once when the stream loop fails abruptly on overflow`, `destroys the source exactly once when a post-loop validation fails`, `still explicitly destroys an undestroyed source on a pre-loop response-header failure`) driving a real `Readable.from` source whose public `destroy()` is counted, so Node's iterator destroy and the client's catch-path teardown are both observable.
- **Recheck correction (authoritative recheck after prior-actor timeout):** the earlier recorded 119/119 focused, 168/168 combined, 150-line, and `0bbea5…`/`862fb5…` figures below were written before the source-destroy-exactly-once tests/guard landed; the verified current truth is 122/122 focused, 171/171 combined ×2, 207 changed lines, and client `sha256:b18645cba9b84ba4f45175cf71f5acdfef20dc540fbed2596bd7a0455dd9e0ab` / spec `sha256:9fa5eaeab38abe61d1ebe9a62551ffef745966a3bb3273cdbf8234f68c2fb37e` (tables below corrected in place).

## WU4B3B2R2 — remediation three-way split, final slice (real mid-stream AbortSignal observation)

**Branch:** `feat/receipt-media-ingestion-wu04b3b2-failure-cleanup` (unchanged) | **Base HEAD:** exact `4036e1145456d5b70a2f279c497e069dff8d4786` (clean tracked baseline: committed R1A `418829f` + R1B `fbe9bd1` + R1B commit `4036e11`; R1B evidence `sha256:5f8c4ed5f95ea12e0cdeada501a24c33a96499d980463ad2998d47272accc5db` preserved untouched) | **Disposition: applied and verified green at 159/400 changed lines; R2 complete; aggregate WU4/WU4B3B2 reconciled after final internal verification (parent owns native token `sha256:265f3d2b…` — native lifecycle untouched).**

### Implemented R2 content (only the real mid-stream signal observation; everything else preserved)

- client (`resolveAndDownload`, stream loop only): an `onAbort` observation registered on `request.signal` immediately before the body loop — on abort after streaming began it destroys the not-yet-destroyed source exactly once with an `ERR_CANCELED`-coded error, so the `for await` loop stops reading/writing promptly and the error maps through the existing `mapTransportError` to the fixed safe `META_TRANSPORT/ABORTED`; `{ once: true }` plus an always-executed `finally` `removeEventListener` guarantees no listener leak on success, failure, pre-abort, or mid-stream abort; the `!source.destroyed` guard preserves the R1B exactly-once source-teardown behavior (an already-destroyed source is never re-destroyed); no raw reason/cause/path/errno/URL/bearer/sender/PII reaches any caller-visible error; coinciding technical-cleanup failures keep the established truthful `FILE_IO_FAILURE` precedence over `ABORTED`.
- spec: the `abortingStream` real-transition source (first read delivers the JPEG chunk; second read aborts the live controller and only then offers a trailing chunk; third read ends the stream), the main RED-first test (`observes a real mid-stream AbortController transition and stops without writing any byte after abort`: fixed safe `ABORTED`, source destroyed exactly once via counted public `destroy()`, agent released once, temp unlinked, write-capture seam proving only `MIN_JPEG` was ever written — the trailing byte is never read/written/hashed), the coincidence test (`surfaces the truthful FILE_IO_FAILURE when a real mid-stream abort and a cleanup failure coincide`, proving requirement-5 precedence with the real transition plus the real `closedFdFactory` EBADF), the success no-listener-leak guard, and one added pre-abort no-leak assertion in the existing already-active-abort test (`getEventListeners(signal, 'abort')` length 0 in every case). The existing injected `ERR_CANCELED` stream-error test remains and is proven adjacent to, not replaced by, the real transition.

## WU4B3B2R2 — pre-registration abort race remediation (narrow remediation WU4B3B2R2-pre-registration-abort-race-remediation)

**Branch:** `feat/receipt-media-ingestion-wu04b3b2-failure-cleanup` (unchanged) | **Base HEAD:** exact `4036e1145456d5b70a2f279c497e069dff8d4786` (committed R1A/R1B + prior R2 candidate preserved) | **Disposition: applied and verified green at 300/400 changed lines; remediates failed independent evidence `sha256:61037c5c1cf69cc9a6a00b8850081060189ef7cc927e744d78e08ca869ad0ad4` (parent owns native token `sha256:745cedb2…` — native lifecycle untouched).**

### Sole blocker (per delegated evidence)

Independent evidence proved that abort during the awaited `tempFileFactory.create()` window occurred **before** the `onAbort` listener was registered, was **not replayed** (Node's EventTarget does not re-fire an `abort` listener added after the signal aborted), and the trailing body chunks were then consumed, written, and hashed, returning `MEDIA_VALIDATION/JPEG_STRUCTURE_INVALID` instead of the fixed safe `META_TRANSPORT/ABORTED`. There was also no post-registration `signal.aborted` re-check.

### Implemented remediation (client, `resolveAndDownload` only)

- The `onAbort` observation is now registered **before the first awaited window that follows the active response body** (the pending `await ioStep(() => this.createTempFile(os.tmpdir()))`), so an abort inside that awaited window destroys the not-yet-consumed source immediately with the `ERR_CANCELED`-coded error and the loop can never consume trailing body bytes.
- An **abort replay re-check** immediately follows registration (registration and re-check share one synchronous block, so no abort can slip between them): an already-fired abort throws the same `ERR_CANCELED`-coded error before any temp file is created.
- The signal is **re-observed after the awaited temp-create resolves** (second `signal.aborted` re-check before the stream loop), so the create-resume window is also closed even if the source destroy path were somehow delayed.
- The single `try { … } finally { removeEventListener }` now spans temp creation, the stream loop, close, read-back, structure validation, settlement, and return, so listener removal is kept on **all registered exits including temp-create failure**; the R1B exactly-once source teardown, single agent release, truthful `FILE_IO_FAILURE` cleanup-failure precedence, and all fixed safe error mappings are unchanged.
- No new helper, no port/validator/policy change, no assertion deletion, no code-golf.

## WU4B3B2R2 — post-temp-create abort recheck remediation (narrow remediation WU4B3B2R2-post-temp-create-abort-recheck-remediation)

**Branch:** `feat/receipt-media-ingestion-wu04b3b2-failure-cleanup` (unchanged) | **Base HEAD:** exact `4036e1145456d5b70a2f279c497e069dff8d4786` (prior pre-registration race remediation candidate preserved, client `sha256:25cb8193…`, spec `sha256:a9e88a89…`, 300/400) | **Disposition: applied and verified green at 389/400 changed lines; remediates failed independent evidence `sha256:5e56ed56cad6a9a92e0a7e841d3529187404685012bcf8b23e89341bb047a0e7` (parent owns native token `sha256:60598f6f…` — native lifecycle untouched).**

### Sole blocker (per delegated evidence)

Failed independent evidence `sha256:5e56ed56…` proved the prior candidate registered the source observation before the temp create and replayed an already-fired abort before creation, but **lacked the promised `signal.aborted` re-check after `await create`**: if the source is already destroyed when the abort fires during the awaited temp-creation window, `onAbort` intentionally does nothing (an already-destroyed source is never re-destroyed), and execution resumed the stream loop on the destroyed source without returning `META_TRANSPORT/ABORTED`.

### Implemented remediation (client, `resolveAndDownload` only)

- **Post-temp-create re-check** immediately after the awaited temp creation/assignment (`temp = tempFile`) and before any stream iteration/write/hash: `if (request.signal.aborted) throw Object.assign(new Error('canceled'), { code: 'ERR_CANCELED' })` — the same safe fixed check as the pre-await replay, deliberately NOT `signal.throwIfAborted()`, because a raw abort reason (`AbortError`) would map to `NETWORK_FAILURE` through `mapTransportError` and would leak the raw reason; the `ERR_CANCELED`-coded throw maps to the fixed safe `META_TRANSPORT/ABORTED`.
- The temp file is claimed (`temp = tempFile`) **before** the re-check throws, so the R1B failure-path close/unlink cleanup still runs and zero residue remains.
- **Catch-path pending-source-error consumer:** with the loop no longer reachable on the post-await throw path, an `ERR_CANCELED` error the `onAbort` observation had already handed to a destroyed source would otherwise surface as an unhandled stream error (the RED-side GREEN run exposed this against the previously green gated abort test). The catch path now attaches a no-op `source.once('error', () => {})` whenever the source is destroyed, so the fixed safe mapping stays the only observable outcome on every post-registration throw path. No mapped outcome, teardown count, listener-removal assertion, or R1A/R1B behavior changed.

## WU4B3B2R2 — source-error-lifecycle remediation (narrow remediation WU4B3B2R2-source-error-lifecycle-remediation)

**Branch:** `feat/receipt-media-ingestion-wu04b3b2-failure-cleanup` (unchanged) | **Base HEAD:** exact `4036e1145456d5b70a2f279c497e069dff8d4786` (prior post-temp-create recheck candidate preserved: client `sha256:afd4df24…`, spec `sha256:a5ac40…`, 389/400) | **Disposition: applied and verified green at exactly 400/400 changed lines; remediates failed independent evidence `sha256:baa6726ba63f10304e61847885fe122331a6dbbefff3909d76bd70cee0fc6d98` (parent owns native token `sha256:1259cfc8…` — native lifecycle untouched).**

### Sole blocker (per delegated evidence)

Failed independent evidence `sha256:baa6726b…` proved the catch-path `source.once('error', () => {})` workaround unsafe on two counts: (1) **too late for delayed temp creation** — a pre-iterator `destroy(ERR_CANCELED)` emits its `'error'` event before the catch consumer can attach, so a gate held past several macrotask turns after a live abort produced an unhandled `ERR_CANCELED`; (2) **persistent on already-destroyed/normal streams** — the once-consumer stayed attached after mid-stream-error, mid-stream-abort, real-transition, coincidence, abrupt-overflow, and post-loop-validation exits (Node's async iterator itself leaves exactly one internal `'error'` handler on consumed paths, so the consumer added a second persistent listener).

### Implemented remediation (client, `resolveAndDownload` only; exactly one honest compact pass)

- **No-error signal destroy:** `onAbort` now destroys the not-yet-destroyed source with `source.destroy()` — no error — so a pre-iterator destroy can never require a late error consumer and no unhandled `ERR_CANCELED` can exist on any awaited window.
- **Fixed-safe boundary checks guarantee ABORTED:** the pre-create replay and post-temp-create re-checks now throw a single shared `canceled()` marker (`ERR_CANCELED`-coded, never `signal.throwIfAborted()`, so no raw abort reason leaks); a **loop-completion boundary check** was added after the `for await` loop (before close/read-back/settlement), and a **catch-boundary check** maps any post-registration live abort to the fixed safe `META_TRANSPORT/ABORTED` before `mapTransportError` — this is what turns the `ERR_STREAM_PREMATURE_CLOSE` rejection of the no-error destroy into the fixed safe ABORTED for both the stalled-next and mid-stream abort cases, without ever surfacing it raw.
- **Catch-path `source.once('error', noop)` consumer removed entirely** (with its stale rationale comment); the R1B exactly-once source teardown guard (`if (source !== undefined && !source.destroyed) source.destroy()`), single agent release, and truthful `FILE_IO_FAILURE` cleanup-failure precedence are unchanged and preserved.
- The old `onAbort`/registration-window remediation comment was rewritten truthfully for the new lifecycle; two duplicated inline `Object.assign` cancellation throws were consolidated into the shared `canceled()` helper (behavior-preserving, no assertion deleted).

## WU4B3B2R2 — post-loop abort-settlement remediation (narrow remediation WU4B3B2R2-post-loop-abort-settlement-remediation)

**Branch:** `feat/receipt-media-ingestion-wu04b3b2-failure-cleanup` (unchanged) | **Base HEAD:** exact `4036e1145456d5b70a2f279c497e069dff8d4786` | **Disposition: applied and verified green at exactly 400/400 changed lines; remediates failed evidence `sha256:2ff77ced903ce21d10230040c6229ca7191e50265a26562fd2debeb0f05084d7` (parent owns native token `sha256:e8abd69dc8d6ddf601dd12057780c4190ece67876ee03e707aeaa7c1513687e6` — native lifecycle untouched, not settled).**

### Sole blocker (per delegated evidence)

Failed evidence `sha256:2ff77ced903ce21d10230040c6229ca7191e50265a26562fd2debeb0f05084d7` proved a post-loop missed-abort window: a live caller abort firing during the awaited success-path temp close or read-back — after the `for await` loop completed normally — false-succeeded, returning a validated file (success result) instead of the fixed safe `META_TRANSPORT/ABORTED`.

### Implemented remediation (client, `resolveAndDownload` only)

- **Post-read-back signal check:** one line-neutrally placed `if (request.signal.aborted) throw canceled();` immediately after the awaited bounded read-back and before MIME validation/`detectMagic`/structure validation/return. Any live abort that fires during the success-path temp close or read-back windows now settles the fixed safe `META_TRANSPORT/ABORTED` via the established shared `canceled()` marker (never `signal.throwIfAborted()`, so no raw abort reason leaks).
- Nothing else changed: the pre-create replay, post-temp-create re-check, loop-completion boundary check, catch-boundary mapping, no-error signal destroy, listener removal in `finally`, exactly-once source teardown guard, and truthful `FILE_IO_FAILURE` cleanup-failure precedence are unchanged and preserved. Comments in the R2 region were compacted only (one honest compact pass; no assertion, case, coverage, or behavior removed).

### WU5A1 coverage remediation (maintainer-authorized, spec-only)

## WU5A2b — structured-field upload taxonomy and CLEANUP_PENDING projection

**Branch:** `feat/receipt-media-ingestion-wu05a2b-failure-taxonomy` | **Base HEAD:** exact `2ed1702acd1143e7eb7baadb9e0a5bff235f2bb5` | **Disposition:** applied and verified green; WU5B and aggregate WU5 remain pending. Remediates the invalidated first WU5A2b attempt (progress-only, no edits, zero evidence); this is native attempt 2 of 2.

### Scope

Only the five authorized paths: `src/receipt-media/domain/object-storage.port.ts`, `src/receipt-media/infrastructure/s3-object-storage.adapter.ts`, `src/receipt-media/infrastructure/s3-object-storage.adapter.spec.ts`, `openspec/changes/receipt-media-ingestion/tasks.md`, `openspec/changes/receipt-media-ingestion/apply-progress.md`.

- **Port:** added fixed codes `HTTP_RETRYABLE`, `HTTP_PERMANENT`, `NETWORK_FAILURE`, `CLEANUP_PENDING`; `ObjectStorageError` now carries deterministic `retryable` (ABORTED/HTTP_RETRYABLE/NETWORK_FAILURE/CLEANUP_PENDING) and `cleanupPending` (CLEANUP_PENDING only) flags derived solely from the code; no cause/stack/message fields.
- **Adapter taxonomy (structured fields only):** HTTP classification from `$metadata.httpStatusCode` — 408, 429, and 500–599 → `HTTP_RETRYABLE`; any other valid status → `HTTP_PERMANENT` (**superseded by WU5A2b-R1/R1b:** valid status is exactly integer 100–599 — 600 and every out-of-range value is present-but-malformed metadata → fixed `PERMANENT_FAILURE`, never `HTTP_PERMANENT`); malformed/missing status → allowlist check of `name` (`NetworkingError`, `TimeoutError`, `SocketTimeoutError`, `AbortError`, `RequestAbortedError`) or `code` (ECONNRESET/ECONNREFUSED/ECONNABORTED/ETIMEDOUT/EPIPE/ENOTFOUND/EAI_AGAIN/EHOSTUNREACH/ENETUNREACH/ENETDOWN) → `NETWORK_FAILURE` (**superseded by WU5A2b-R1/R1b:** only absent `$metadata` reaches the allowlist — present-but-invalid metadata, including malformed status, is always fixed `PERMANENT_FAILURE`, never allowlist fall-through); else fixed `PERMANENT_FAILURE`. Real caller abort keeps precedence → fixed `ABORTED` (retryable). Messages, stacks, and raw error objects are never read, parsed, or retained.
- **Cleanup projection:** after upload starts, a cleanup signal already aborted at compensation time or a throwing DeleteObject yields exactly fixed `CLEANUP_PENDING` (`retryable=true`, `cleanupPending=true`), hiding both the original and cleanup failures; a successful same-key compensation preserves the original safely classified failure and its flags. Cleanup missing/aliased/pre-aborted before upload remains pre-send `REQUEST_INVALID` (zero sends).
- **Preserved:** distinct upload/cleanup signals, pre-invalid rejection matrix, stream settlement/listener teardown, exact byte/max/SHA-256 verification, same-key compensation on every post-send failure, success never deletes, all 20 committed WU5A1/WU5A2a tests (three had classification expectations updated to the new fixed taxonomy — the WU5A2b task itself; no assertion coverage removed).

### WU5A2b-R1b adversarial-signal remediation (maintainer-authorized, remediates every non-code R2 finding in failed independent evidence `sha256:25da7c1442b9a5187fdadf08a1824ca12146418c771f4f2b6a1d0c043165440d`)

**Branch/HEAD unchanged:** `feat/receipt-media-ingestion-wu05a2b-failure-taxonomy` / `2ed1702acd1143e7eb7baadb9e0a5bff235f2bb5`. **R1b native begin tree = the preserved R1 candidate bytes** (adapter `sha256:bc209ae1…`, spec `sha256:f43e9c85…`, tasks `sha256:cafd169f…`, progress `sha256:494da361…`; port byte-identical `sha256:9d526aaa…`, never edited in R1b). Compaction passes used in R1b: **0** (none authorized, none needed). No stage/commit/push/PR/review/merge/release; no real AWS/network/credentials; no native attempt tokens persisted.

- **Code finding (adversarial signals):** replaced the R1 duck-typed shape gate with a minimal fail-closed genuine-AbortSignal boundary (`GuardedSignal`/`guardSignal`): only `v instanceof AbortSignal` instances whose single fail-closed `aborted` read yields a boolean are accepted. Callable-looking forged methods that throw, method getters whose value changes after validation, delayed/stateful `aborted` accessors, proxies, and prototype spoofs all fail fixed `REQUEST_INVALID` before any S3 send, pipeline start, or listener registration — zero sends, no raw error/message/cause/stack leakage. The entry `aborted` state is captured exactly once; later abort detection (classify, post-send, cleanup-pending) uses only the adapter's own guarded abort listeners (`callerAborted`/`cleanupAborted` flags), never re-invoking attacker-controlled getters. Guarded listener add is fail-closed (`REQUEST_INVALID` before side effects, partial registration unwound) and removal is swallowed so `finally` teardown can never override a safe projection. The caller's exact signal object is passed to AWS (referential identity preserved in both Put and Delete SDK options).
- **Preserved:** all 26 prior tests and assertions unchanged; valid pre-aborted upload → `ABORTED`; missing/aliased/pre-aborted cleanup → `REQUEST_INVALID`; real mid-stream/post-send caller abort → `ABORTED` with same-key DeleteObject compensation; valid cleanup aborted after upload start → `CLEANUP_PENDING`; full WU5A1/WU5A2a/WU5A2b taxonomy, stream settlement, integrity, compensation, and no-delete-success behavior.
- **Accounting finding (corrected in place in the R1 section above):** the R1 delta was miscomputed as 106; measured against the preserved baseline tree `329c9bc645d1e636107a81c5c78d3a501dcc36b9` (blob hashes verified identical to the recorded baseline bytes) it is exactly **120/400** (adapter 37 = 31+/6−; spec 39 = 38+/1−; tasks.md 2 = 1+/1−; apply-progress.md 42 = 42+/0−). The cumulative HEAD-relative disclosure (495 = pre-R1 candidate 389 + 106 HEAD-relative increment) remains accurate — the 120 tree-relative delta and the 106 HEAD-relative increment differ because several R1 edits rewrote lines already counted in the pre-R1 candidate diff; both measurements are disclosed, and the tree-relative 120/400 is the authoritative R1 budget accounting.
- **Prose findings (superseded in place in the WU5A2b section above and in `tasks.md`):** earlier text describing statuses ≥600 as valid/`HTTP_PERMANENT` and malformed-status metadata falling through to the network allowlist is explicitly superseded: valid HTTP status is exactly integer 100–599, and only absent `$metadata` reaches the allowlisted `name`/`code` classification — present-but-invalid metadata (including malformed/out-of-range status) is always fixed `PERMANENT_FAILURE`.
- **Provisional closure:** WU5A2b closure remains provisional until R2b independent reverification passes; WU5B and aggregate WU5 remain pending and untouched.

#### Preserved historical RED (never rerun, never recreated)

The first R1c actor's spec-only Proxy test failed unchanged (old) production exactly **1 failed / 27 passed / 28 total** — a Proxy around a genuine AbortSignal that spoofs `aborted` and listener methods received an upload success on the R1b production, proving the R1b genuine-instance boundary still admitted proxies. That RED is valid and preserved. The same actor's partial GREEN was **invalid**: the adapter imported a nonexistent top-level `isProxy` from `node:util`, so the brand-gate call threw for every ordinary genuine signal and the whole boundary fail-closed to `REQUEST_INVALID` — the recorded failed run was **20 failed / 8 passed** (`sha256:6bf780badaf31b93210228811e7802d1954f43b0cdc16b3645fbc1a7e7df0bca`). This continuation moved from the preserved RED straight to GREEN; no RED was rerun or recreated.

#### Minimal GREEN (adapter + spec only)

- Replaced the nonexistent import with the portable Node API: `import { types as utilTypes } from 'node:util'` and `utilTypes.isProxy(v)`.
- The proxy brand check now runs strictly **before** `instanceof AbortSignal`, so a proxy's `getPrototypeOf` trap can never run; proxies around genuine signals fail closed to fixed `REQUEST_INVALID` before any property access or side effect.
- Retained the compact captured native AbortSignal `aborted` getter and EventTarget add/remove calls (prototype-captured, invoked with the validated signal as receiver) so attacker-shadowed instance properties are never dynamically read or invoked; corrected the earlier doc claims that this runtime's native bindings unwrap proxy receivers — the captured native calls bypass instance shadowing but **do not by themselves reject proxies**; the `util.types.isProxy` brand gate is the proxy rejection.
- All native calls stay inside fail-closed handling; listener-removal failures remain swallowed in `finally` and can never override a safe result.
- Spec: the preserved Proxy test is behaviorally unchanged — proxied genuine upload and cleanup signals each independently receive `REQUEST_INVALID` with zero sends and no raw trap leakage; only the `Reflect.get` line was typed (`const v: unknown`, `(v as () => unknown).bind(target)`) to clear 4 legitimate `@typescript-eslint/no-unsafe-*` errors in the added test. `AbortSignal` subclassing is not constructible in this runtime (`Illegal constructor`, verified), so genuine real-signal identity reaching AWS exactly is covered by the preserved exact-identity test (`sent[0].opts.abortSignal` is the caller's signal object).
- All 27 prior tests preserved with WU5A1/WU5A2a/provider taxonomy/CLEANUP_PENDING semantics exactly intact; the combined proxy test already proves cleanup independently, so no split/table was needed (28 total tests).

### WU6A — capability token primitive (complete/provisional; independent verification pending)

Pure constructor-input `CapabilityService` at `src/receipt-media/application/capability.service.ts` (+ spec): deterministic HMAC-SHA256 issuance over `receipt-media-capability:v1:<uuid>` with an independently precomputed fixed vector, strict 43-char canonical base64url parsing (padding/whitespace/foreign alphabet/length/noncanonical trailing bits, incl. a token decoding to identical bytes), SHA-256 ASCII lookup-input production, fixed 32-byte stored-or-dummy `timingSafeEqual` (pass-through module spy observes the actual 32-byte dummy comparison for missing and wrong-length hashes), defensive key-buffer copies, and version-safe reconstruction failing closed on absent/replaced historical keys, tampered hashes, and non-canonical uuids/versions with fixed secret-free error constants. Bounded WU6A malformed stored-hash remediation (maintainer-authorized reset, `WU6A-proxy-hash-remediation`): the `instanceof`+`.length` `isHashLength` guard was replaced by fail-closed `asHashCandidate` — an `Object.prototype.toString` internal-slot check that Proxy exotic objects cannot spoof, plus a try-guarded length check and defensive copy — so proxied/throwing stored hashes for a valid token fail closed with exactly one genuine 32-byte dummy comparison and no raw error; valid Buffer/Uint8Array and malformed-token no-comparison behavior preserved. Fresh bounded `WU6A-symbol-tostringtag-remediation` (maintainer-authorized; supersedes the unspoofable-tag claim earlier in this section): the toString tag read now sits inside the try with a new genuine-brand `ArrayBuffer.isView` internal-slot check; `Symbol.toStringTag` is spoofable and `Buffer.from` accepts spoofed-tag array-likes, so the isView check (which no Proxy/spoof can pass and which reads no attacker property) gates comparison-byte supply.

TDD: RED first — focused suite failed 1 suite/0 tests (`Cannot find module './capability.service'`) on unchanged production; GREEN 21/21, then consolidated to 11/11 with every assertion retained (fixtures corrected: `9000` uuid variant is legitimately valid per RFC 4122 and was replaced with `c000`; all-digit `UUID_A.toUpperCase()` no-op replaced with a letter-bearing uuid). Fresh corrective RED (distinct from the historical module-not-found RED above; production unchanged before it): regression added for proxied stored hashes — ordinary transparent `new Proxy(Buffer.alloc(32), {})`, hostile throwing-`length` proxy, `reconstruct()` with a hostile proxy, and valid `new Uint8Array(VECTOR_HASH)` preservation — and the suite failed 1 test/11 passed with `TypeError: Method get TypedArray.prototype.length called on incompatible receiver [object Uint8Array]` escaping `verify()` at `isHashLength` (`capability.service.ts:30:40`); after the smallest fail-closed production correction the same command passed 12/12. TRIANGULATE: additive rotation with old-version reconstruction, absent/replaced-key fail-closed, restart determinism, dummy-comparison call-shape assertions (exactly one genuine 32-byte zero-dummy comparison per proxied-hash call), ordinary vs hostile proxy, valid Buffer (vector tests) and valid Uint8Array true. REFACTOR: service kept thin/port-free; no structural refactor required beyond the minimal correction; controller, revocation, and lookup deliberately untouched for later WU6. Fresh corrective RED (third distinct RED; `WU6A-symbol-tostringtag-remediation`; production unchanged before it): the hostile trap was tightened to throw on EVERY property (subsumes throwing-length; exposes `Symbol.toStringTag` throw) and the suite failed 1 failed/11 passed with `Error: hostile get trap` escaping `verify()` at the outside-try `Object.prototype.toString.call` (`capability.service.ts:35:33`) — distinct from the historical module-not-found RED and the prior transparent-proxy incompatible-receiver RED. GREEN: smallest correction moved all candidate recognition (toString gate, new genuine-brand `ArrayBuffer.isView`, length check, defensive copy) inside the single try; `SPOOF` (`{[Symbol.toStringTag]:'Uint8Array', length:32}`, passes the toString gate) added to the bad array and the test renamed to cover spoofing; same command then passed 12/12. TRIANGULATE: transparent proxied Buffer (rejected by the toString gate), all-properties-throwing proxy (throw caught → dummy), spoofed-tag array-like (isView rejects → dummy), valid `new Uint8Array(VECTOR_HASH)` true, valid Buffer via vector tests, `reconstruct(UUID_A, 1, hostile)` null, exactly one genuine 32-byte dummy `timingSafeEqual` per fail call, malformed-token no-comparison preserved.
